# Kanban and task specifications
Updated 2026-10-14. This file is the canonical task tracker.

**Current scope: GitHub main Build 020.** Use [the documentation authority guide](00-documentation-guide.md) to distinguish owner targets, committed source, historical build evidence and Studio/release acceptance. [Build 020 integration checklist](44-main-integration-checklist.md) records bounded passes and remaining gates, but **this file alone owns task statuses**. GitHub integration does not publish Roblox or certify paid items, physical-device performance or newest persistent migration.

**Historical milestone summary:** Builds 005â€“013 established PvP, storage, collection, old direct-copy reward tests, earned chests, Gold and original procedural visuals. Those older checks are *not* proof the latest reward vision is implemented. For actual per-build acceptance consult the dated sections and historical evidence below. Physical-phone/mobile acceptance remains open, though earlier phone-emulator checks passed.

## Board counts

Task counts are **not stored here**. A generated table inside the one file every PR edits conflicted on almost every merge, and the refresh bot could not push to the protected `main`. Run `python tools/board_summary.py` for the live table; CI also prints it in each run's job summary. Never paste counts into this file.

## Owner decisions log 2026-10-16

Dated decisions from this session (sources in brackets). "Lead decision" means made on the owner's behalf; the owner may overrule. Everything below is OFF by default behind flags and is **Review / test or Backlog, never Done**: no phone, 12-player or owner-playtest evidence exists yet.

- **Low-poly base dinos** (BD-224, BD-225, BD-232): one faceted low-poly base look per species from Roblox's mesh generator, split into parts; first spike Compy/Raptor/Triceratops, registry now covers all 22 species. Flag off. Skins (Plushy, Clay, Bricks) are a later paid cosmetic layer with gentle defeat animations ([97](97-dino-skins.md)).
- **Generated meshes only**: no code-built 3D models as final art; 2D art from Blender or the owner ([87](87-vision-and-feel-guide.md), [72](72-ui-copy-and-asset-standards.md)). **Scoped exception (owner 2026-10-16, BD-462, [140](140-tree-quality-test.md)):** the three Blender-scripted V3 trees (broadleaf, conifer, palm) are approved as final world art after the owner saw the renders; the exception covers ONLY those three meshes and is not a general permission. ## Owner decisions log 2026-10-17

- **Gifting to ANYONE** (owner 2026-10-17, overturns the LEAD's docs/134 Q1 "friends only"; [115](115-decisions-log.md) "OWNER decision 2026-10-17: gifting to anyone", [134](134-gifting-spec.md) s10 Q1): a player can gift any Roblox user, in the lobby, online elsewhere, offline by username, or someone who has never played Be Dino. Recipient picker = server players plus a username search (`Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync`, display name + avatar confirm before paying). Offline delivery = server-side Gifts inbox claimed on next join, exactly once and idempotent; a never-played recipient's gift waits with no expiry (LEAD recommendation). Safety recheck for strangers (docs/134 s2.1): preset messages only, recipient setting "Gifts from: Everyone (default) / Friends / Nobody" with an easy off (Settings, popup, Mute sender), blocked users cannot gift, rate limits, sender display name shown (no anonymity), receipt-idempotent gift product, admin Revoke for refunds, audit by UserId. **All Robux flags stay off; enabling needs the owner's explicit go-ahead** (docs/134 s12 go-live list). Rows BD-451, BD-452, BD-453 updated; priorities unchanged (P2, P2, P3).

## Operating rules
- Preserve task IDs and record the commit/build or actual test evidence used for completion.
- Server authority and persistence safety are P0. Do not trade these for polish.
- Proposed balance stays configurable until Znypr approves it.
- Znypr performs Studio/account/device actions unavailable to this environment.
- Do not maintain a second conflicting task board.
- **Picking numbers (parallel agents).** Before you add a row, run `python tools/next_bd_id.py` (several rows: `--reserve N`) and use exactly the id it prints. It looks at `origin/main`, every open PR branch and the other worktrees on this machine, and reserves the id for 48 hours, so two agents cannot get the same one. Same for docs: `python tools/next_bd_id.py --doc`. Do not guess "highest + 1" from your own copy. Two CI checks guard the file: `tools/verify_doc_ids.py` (no duplicate ids) and `tools/verify_kanban_merge.py` (fails a PR that reuses an id `main` already gave to a different task, or puts back an older copy of a row; its message lists the rows to take from `main`, and `--fix` does it). When merging `main` into your branch, take `main`'s version of every row you did not change yourself.

### BD-044: Owner-approved reward-loop correction (2026-10-08)

**Status: Review / test (2026-10-13). Implemented on `claude/bd-044-reward-loop`; offline Luau evidence only, Studio verification pending. P0 before economy/public release.**

**Implementation 2026-10-13** ([details and evidence](50-bd044-reward-loop.md)): owner decisions of 2026-10-13 applied: one banked run egg per catch (15/150 at 500/5,000 food), exactly one dinosaur per successful egg, no `N(c)=c` copies or threshold eggs; server-side ordered run-egg bank behind the five-slot queue with exactly-once settlement (`settledRuns` + operation log, deterministic egg IDs, precommitted outcomes); PROVISIONAL deterministic run crystals (30/219); separate five bonus-nest slots with visible pending nests and a run-start gate instead of loss; additive schema, old queued eggs still claimable. New `tools/verify_rewards.py` in CI. Still open: Studio/device/multiplayer acceptance, 150-egg pacing UX, Uncommon/Epic taxonomy, balance sign-off. Checklist items below stay unchecked until Studio evidence exists.
The newer owner's reward intent supersedes the 2026-09-17 test economy's `N(c)=c` immediate dinosaur copies and the 10/100/500 catches -> 1/2/3 extra-egg thresholds. [Canonical target and illustrated distributions](09-reward-economy.md) and [AI/contributor instructions](../AGENTS.md) define the intended behavior; [actual Build 020](44-main-integration-checklist.md) is the separate implementation record.

**Acceptance checklist (do not check off from documentation alone):**

- [ ] 500 **food** points -> example 15 catches -> **15 banked eggs** (NOT 15 guaranteed Compy copies + 1/2 threshold eggs). 5,000 **food** points -> example 150 catches -> **150 run eggs**; distinguish from historical 5,000 catch-score cap and test 0/negative/overflow values.
- [ ] Run return/death/disconnect settle each egg exactly once, with immutable random species/rarity/condition/genetic/trait outcomes, saved batches, correct event provenance, Cracked failure and legacy data migration. No direct-copy double credit or preserved duplicate exploit.
- [ ] Hatch every run egg individually in increasing species rarity, at 1 second/egg (owner decision 2026-10-13, was ~5 s in the first prototype). 15-egg and 150-egg cases: clear progress, close/skip/resume, mobile responsiveness, cancellation and performance. Define 150-egg pacing alternative with owner before launch.
- [ ] **Rarity taxonomy migration** (code + offline tests done 2026-10-14, [82](82-species-rarity-tiers.md); Studio/phone pending): current species tiers are only Common/Rare/Legendary; owner examples need Uncommon/Epic as distinct species rarity categories. Design tier mapping, eligible catalog species, data/UI color names, saved rarity ordering, rewards, leaderboards and tests before promising the examples. The sample 15-egg result can be 11 Common/4 Uncommon; the 150-egg result can be 80 Common/50 Uncommon/17 Rare/3 Epic. These **are not mandatory quotas or fixed odds**; eligibility/distribution must be configurable and fair.
- [ ] Grant crystals independently from catches and map pickups; prototype example **30** run crystals at 500 food is assistant illustrative ONLY, **220** at 5,000 food is the owner's illustrative example. Define/approve actual earning and crystal-box rules separately.
- [ ] Model **bonus nests** as separate rewards from individual caught eggs, with **five nest inventory/claim spots**, not the existing ordinary egg queue capacity. Example: 1 nest from 500 food; 6 nests offered from 5,000 food, only 5 claimable if all five slots are free. Explicitly resolve where the sixth offered nest goes (pending/blocked/expiry) and prevent silent reward loss; define nest timing/content.
- [ ] Retune and test quantity (currently 1/2/3 copies per egg), species odds, egg outcomes/disclosure, fusion pacing, 150+ egg batch payloads and reward caps. Old `tools/economy_sim.py`, `tests/test_economy.py`, Config thresholds and Studio fixtures remain **historical tests** until deliberately replaced.
- [ ] End-to-end multiplayer/rejoin/persistence, queue limits, migration, real-device interaction, UI and no paid-random regression verified with evidence before marking done. Do not touch persistent profiles or enable paid random items without a separate release review.

Rather than duplicate every subtask here, use the detailed [categorized owner feedback specification](45-owner-feedback-shop-hud-world-weather-tasks.md) for acceptance checklists, source references and design constraints.

Studio verification pending. [Evidence](50-bd044-reward-loop.md#bd-045-condition-odds-bd045-conditions-v1).

Offline tests only; Studio checks and the owner Android checklist in doc 51 are the remaining gates. BD-054 dependency BD-046 (Shop entry) is not required for this layout pass.

New **Cracked 50%** selection combined with existing **80% failure on cracked** implies **40% total failed eggs at tier 0**, a high-risk balancing review.

### BD-057â€“BD-065: Pattern fusion, permanent dinosaur identity and earned prestige (2026-10-08)

**OWNER TARGET; only the disabled BD-057 Phase 1 transaction foundation exists ([49](49-identity-fusion-phase1.md)); task index above controls status.** Gold consumes **10 same-species + same-egg-pattern dinosaur instances** (example ten Wavy-Stripes Compies) and guarantees **ONE NEW Gold Wavy-Stripes Compy**; it **does NOT preserve the traits of a selected hero**. The new result's colors, condition, Shiny, event mutation and BIG are **rolled from versioned ingredient-dependent probabilities**. Pattern remains fixed by the same-pattern recipe. **Emerald comes after Gold and before Diamond**; higher-stage costs, additional materials and Obsidian/Celestial are proposals. Fusion always grants the guaranteed higher stage/stat improvement, never a random chance to lose all ingredients with nothing.

Each hatched dinosaur keeps **its immutable originating egg record**, but the **NEW fusion output** instead stores **a parent-dino/source-egg lineage** and freshly rolled traits. A living dino can purchase many aura/trail options and switch between them; on fusion **all input dinos and their aura/trail purchases are consumed, with explicit loss confirmation**, and no bought cosmetics transfer to the new result. A dedicated **Fusion Menu** must sort/filter by species, Shiny, mutation, colors, pattern, condition and other traits, display selected ingredients, exact chances and input loss, then reveal each **server-committed trait** via entertaining, skippable wheel animations. **2/10 Shiny ingredients â†’ 10% Shiny on result** is a **proposed formula**, not approved balance. Show true **hatch odds only for hatched dinos** versus **conditional fusion-outcome odds for fused ones**; earned tier is not a random drop %. Bought cosmetics are excluded. Blender Gold/Emerald/Diamond subtle/medium surfaces must preserve **the newly rolled output** DNA and support stacked new effects.

Complete ownership, recipes, safe handling of valuable donors/purchased cosmetics, **ingredient-dependent RNG and transparent wheel reveal (BD-064â€“065)**, deterministic duplicate-sink proposal (BD-063), lineage/migration, earned stats, Blender materials, multi-week balancing and tests are specified **only** in [the advanced fusion specification](46-pattern-fusion-dino-identity-and-cosmetic-progression.md). Roblox [paid-random rules](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-random-items) can cover synthesis when ingredients are Robux-linked; trace and restrict eligible inputs and keep paid flags disabled until approved. Build 020's 50/50 fusion remains current code, not new owner goal.

| Desktop/phone icon audit | Archived screenshots show illustrated trophy, navigation and currency loading; iPhone 17 Pro landscape and desktop layouts inspected | Pass for tested layouts; small modal touch targets remain an ergonomics limitation |
| UI regressions | Claimed-egg success toast covered the preview: suppressed redundant toast; growth pulse targeted navigation scale: now restores HUD's own responsive base; real phone-emulated food pickup yielded 5 growth with HUD scale 0.47625 / rail 1 | Fixed, synced and retested |

Screenshot PNGs and structured runtime traces are archived in
[`docs/evidence/2026-10-08-icons-and-acceptance/`](evidence/2026-10-08-icons-and-acceptance/README.md).
Species screenshots show actual equipped models in Sanctuary after their movement
runs; they use a temporary inspection camera. Initial hatch captures preserve the
toast defect, and the final hatch capture verifies its correction. Keyboard selection
activated UI buttons; Escape is blocked by Studio VirtualInput, so close buttons were
used instead. This is not proof of real finger interaction.

Validation: 43 Luau sources compile, all 21 Python tests pass, binding checks and
artwork assembly pass, and the standard build/source round-trip succeeds. No gameplay
script errors were observed; Assistant camera-reset messages are tool diagnostics.
Still unverified: real target-phone performance/touch ergonomics, two-client interaction
and races/load, published non-owner permissions, production persistence/rejoin of the
new transactions and experience privacy/access settings. Historical Done entries are
not extended to those cases; BD-019 and the larger device/multiplayer gates stay open.
Both instances were left stopped; default viewport restored. The disposable source
fixture was removed after testing. Fourteen archived PNGs passed image verification.

### BD-001 â€” research
Done. Initial screenshot/source research and planning documents were completed on 2026-09-17.

### BD-002 â€” direction
Done. Znypr selected a private test, cute/simple ground arena and small complete progression loop.

### BD-003 â€” setup/design blockers
Done. Personal account, Studio installed, â‚¬0 target budget, immediate run dinosaur loot plus separate chest loot, increasing total copy rewards and stacked duplicates are confirmed.

### BD-004 â€” reproducible project/private place
**Done when:** source mapping/build is reproducible and the actual private test experience/place is recorded with isolated test data.

Source mapping, dependency-free Python packager and generated `.rbxlx` are working. A separate private test experience is now being used for DataStore testing, but the repository still does not record its experience/place IDs. That remains the blocker for closing BD-004.

2026-10-13 (Milestone 1): the playtest uses a **new** private experience with a fresh DataStore; IDs are pending from Znypr. Record them in `PlaytestUniverseId` / `PlaytestPlaceId` in `src/shared/Config.luau` (placeholders marked `TODO(BD-004)`, right after `ProfileDataStoreName`) and in the release record of the [playtest runbook](55-playtest-release-runbook.md) Â§2/Â§6. `tools/verify_release.py --profile playtest` and `tools/build.py --profile playtest` fail until both are positive. Move to Done when that commit lands.

### BD-005 â€” persistence and remote contracts
Done as a specification. `docs/13-persistence-remote-contracts.md` chooses a project-owned `DataStoreService`/`UpdateAsync` repository with leases, revisions, immutable operation outcomes and explicit remote validation. Durable implementation remains BD-011.

### BD-006 â€” controller/camera
**Done when:** desktop and touch movement work, min/max visual-size behavior is usable and two clients replicate correctly.

Desktop evidence passed: movement, camera, dinosaur visibility, size behavior, reset isolation and two-client replication. Mobile/touch is still untested, so the task remains `Review / test`.

2026-10-13 note: the touch HUD/control layout (BD-054), hill traction (BD-053) and Home (BD-052) changes in [51](51-bd053-054-movement-mobile.md) include the owner Android checklist that can also supply BD-006 mobile evidence.

### BD-007 â€” asset kit
**Done when:** one original dinosaur silhouette/palette, manifest template and procedural fallback are reviewable.

Done. `docs/23-original-asset-manifest.md` records the original procedural silhouettes, palettes, Gold treatment, arena kit, rights/source and zero external-asset dependency used by Build 013.

### BD-008 â€” food/growth
Done. Znypr reported all Build 004 checklist cases working: pickup, growth, respawn, simultaneous ownership, remote growth replication and reset. The implementation remains bounded and server-owned; tuning is temporary.

### BD-009 â€” PvP/spawn safety/run ending
Done for desktop multiplayer. Znypr reported Build 005 tests 1â€“9 all working: spawn protection, standing-still exit, movement cancellation, equal-size safety, bigger-eats-smaller, protection after respawn, replication, exit-vs-predation race and Reset Character. The shared terminal guard produced no reported duplicate endings.

### BD-010 â€” alpha economy
Done for private mechanics testing. `tools/economy_sim.py`, `tests/test_economy.py` and `docs/15-economy-simulation.md` define and verify the 0â€“5000 catch range, exact increasing copy totals, exponential rarity allocation, eligibility thresholds, independent chest grants, repeat-victim anti-farm proposal and 50-copy mutation risk.

Important finding: one Common alpha species reaches 50 copies at catch score 62. The 50-copy mutation cost is therefore a mechanics-test value, not accepted public progression balance.

### BD-011 â€” persistence/settlement
**Done when:** rejoin, load failures, duplicate settlement, stale sessions and competing servers preserve committed inventory and immutable run outcomes.

Build 006 runtime tests 1â€“10 passed. Confirmed: load/save/rejoin, exact +1 reward settlement, deliberate duplicate settlement does not double-grant, Reset Character settles the run, repeat-victim protection behaves as designed, disabling Studio API access fails closed with no playable dinosaur, and re-enabling access restores the prior profile. Znypr also confirmed Compy copies can increase above 3; the earlier apparent stop was the 60-second anti-farm window rather than a cap.

Build 007 completed the final acceptance case. Znypr confirmed the lease test passed in a new private test experience, earned copies persisted through a full restart, and the probe did not corrupt player progression. BD-011 is Done. The temporary lease probe is removed from normal runtime in Build 008.

### BD-012 â€” collection/equip
**Done when:** three species definitions, owned/locked counts and equip survive reconnect; invalid equip is rejected.

Build 008 passed all eight runtime checks. Owned/locked counts, server rejection of locked T-Rex, one-time Triceratops test unlock, visual switching, Reset Character persistence and full reconnect persistence all worked. BD-012 is Done.

### BD-013 â€” earned chest queue
**Done when:** server timestamps, offline elapsed time, duplicate claims and capacity/overflow policy pass. Chest loot remains independent from immediate run loot.

Build 009 passed all eight runtime checks. The five-slot active queue, two-item overflow case, absolute offline timestamps, claim persistence, duplicate-claim protection and duplicate test-grant protection all worked. BD-013 is Done.

### BD-014 â€” Gold mutation
**Done when:** atomic 50-copy conversion handles 49/50/51 copies, duplicate requests and equipped-copy semantics without negative counts.

Build 010 passed all eight runtime checks. The 49-copy rejection, exact 50 and 51 conversions, duplicate mutation replay, equipped Gold-only semantics, Gold visual and reconnect persistence all worked. BD-014 is Done.

### BD-015 â€” core UI
**Done when:** lobby, HUD, run summary, collection and chest queue are usable on desktop/touch and large duplicate stacks render as grouped counts rather than hundreds of cards.

Build 011 passed all eight checks. Tutorial, compact HUD, grouped collection rows, chest UI, Home, run summary and phone-landscape emulator layout/actions all worked, with development grant/setup controls absent. BD-015 is Done.

### BD-016 â€” dinosaur/map kit
**Done when:** three distinct original dinosaurs, one arena and Gold treatment pass collision/camera/permission checks. Procedural fallback is acceptable for the private test.

Build 013 integrates the original procedural Compy, Triceratops, T-Rex, Gold treatment and meadow/nest environment kit. All decorative props are non-colliding and no external asset permissions are required. See `docs/24-visual-kit-test.md`.

### BD-017 â€” audio
**Done when:** minimal effects work for a non-owner published tester with independent mute/volume behavior. Ambience is optional.

### BD-018 â€” multiplayer/security/persistence suite
Execute the verification matrix from `docs/06-roadmap-and-testing.md`. Source inspection is not runtime evidence.

Build 012 passed all eight runtime checks. Malformed/flooded remotes, impossible movement correction, normal gameplay after attacks, two-client isolation, equal-size safety, one predation outcome, end-run-vs-predation terminal race and post-restart inventory persistence all passed. BD-018 is Done.

### BD-019 â€” device/load/published assets
Requires a real target phone plus the agreed multiplayer/pickup load test and non-owner asset checks.

2026-10-13 â€” **In progress, owner publish pending.** `tools/verify_load.py` (in CI) runs the real ProfileRepository with a mocked, latency/throttle-modelled DataStore: 8 and 12 simulated players over 15 min pass with zero failed operations, no throttling and peak 55â€“57% of the 60 + 10Ã—players/min budget. Report-only: a BD-044 150-egg simultaneous reveal sits at ~100% of budget and one equip-spamming client uses ~60% of an 8-player server's budget; batching egg claims and a per-player write budget are recommended. The Studio 2/4/8-client, desktop + Android and non-owner rows of the [runbook matrix](55-playtest-release-runbook.md) Â§5 remain to be executed after publish. Offline results are not device or live-DataStore evidence.

### BD-020 â€” private release/rollback
Requires a known-good commit/build, private access settings, no public debug grants, known issues and a tested rollback.

2026-10-13 â€” **Review / test, owner publish pending.** `python tools/build.py --profile playtest` produces a reproducible playtest place (playtest store name, `ReleaseProfile = "playtest"`, manifest with commit/dirty flag) and `tools/verify_release.py` enforces paid/test/debug flags off (default profile in CI). [Playtest runbook](55-playtest-release-runbook.md) holds gates, owner Studio publish steps, tagging (`playtest-m1-bNNN-rK`), release record, known-issues template, rollback/store-rotation and data-reset policy. Done requires the actual private publish, the recorded release row and a performed rollback drill.

### BD-021 â€” observed community test
Run the small invited session only after BD-020. Capture friction, repeat-run behavior and defects separately from suggestions.

### BD-022 â€” public-alpha decision
Public scope is an evidence-based later decision. Passing code generation alone never triggers launch.

Studio repro on the playtest build: after the server teleported the client-owned character to the island at run start, it could drop straight through the terrain as it began to walk, then MovementGuard kept snapping the falling root back to mid-fall baselines (endless fall to -500). Fix: characters load straight into the hub, only after terrain + hub exist; a run starts only after the server terrain is built and the client reports its own terrain under the spawn as loaded (20 s fallback); the run-start teleport takes physics ownership first; FallRecovery returns any root 20 studs under the floor to the island spawn (run) or hub, and MovementGuard leaves fallen roots to it. Shared SpawnGeometry; offline tools/verify_spawn.py. Studio: two fresh playtest sessions walked 4 directions with no fall; a forced under-map drop recovered in 0.25 s. Flows through `ResourceModels.clone`, so playable dino, shop/Index/hatch previews all use it. Verified: offline `tools/verify_lowpoly_dinos.py`; Studio fresh build with flag on, `docs/evidence/2026-10-16-lowpoly-spike/` (idle, walk, 3 previews, gene tint). NOT verified: phone, published place, multiplayer. FIX 2026-10-16 (branch claude/lowpoly-keep-detail): gene colours no longer flatten the model. Findings: each part is a plain MeshPart with its own 1024px TextureID (no SurfaceAppearance, no vertex colours) and MeshPart.Color is ignored while a texture is set, so a Color tint does nothing and clearing the texture lost eyes and belly. Now the original texture stays and `LowPolyTexture` bakes a 256px gene-coloured EditableImage per part (cached per texture and colour) using the pure `LowPolyRecolor` pixel rule: only saturated, bright body-colour pixels are recoloured (shade kept), black eyes/claws and the cream belly pass through untouched; if the bake fails the original species-coloured texture stays. Matte kept. Offline-tested (`tools/verify_lowpoly_dinos.py`); Studio screenshots `gene-tint-keeps-detail-1/2.jpg` (Compy, Raptor, Triceratops, three gene sets). NOT verified: a published live server (EditableImage from asset needs the place/creator to own the textures), phone cost of the one-off bakes, Gold/Diamond look. Known gaps: patterns not painted, PatternGenes bypassed for low-poly; no Gold/Diamond look check. REGISTRY 2026-10-16 (branch claude/lowpoly-registry-22, flag still off, registry only): all 22 shipped species now have their published generate_mesh id in `LowPolyDinoAssets` (19 new, docs/77 lines), each with tier and class (Ground/Fly/Water/Armored, docs/101) and a size `scale = 2.0 * 1.1^(tier-1) * shape` (about 10% per tier; shapes: Brachiosaurus 1.6, T-Rex 1.25, Spinosaurus/Therizinosaurus/Stegosaurus 1.2, Compy/Archaeopteryx/Microraptor small, fly and water near 1; table in the module header). Part matching moved to the pure `LowPolyPose.roleOf` (adds wing, flipper, neck; Compy/Raptor/Triceratops unchanged); `LowPolyPose.sample` takes an optional class: Fly wings stay folded (even under Reduce Motion), Water sits lower with shorter strokes, Armored shorter steps. Verified offline only: `tools/verify_lowpoly_dinos.py` (22 species mapped to the expected ids, unique, tier/class match config, scale grows per tier, part-name sets per body plan, poses finite/bounded). NOT verified: no Studio was open, so none of the 19 new ids was loaded, and the real part names were not read (the matcher is checked against the expected name sets: quad/biped/wings/flippers/neck); per-species Recolor base colour is not measured (new species use the default grey base, so gene recolour may look wrong); sizes and fold angle never seen on screen; not on phone PATTERNS + TIERS 2026-10-16 (branch claude/lowpoly-patterns-tiers, flag still off, docs/105): spots/stripes now paint on low-poly. New pure `LowPolyPattern` (same 8 gene pattern ids, two gene colours, blend = share, deterministic): the runtime rasterises each part's triangles into UV space once (EditableMesh -> position map) and the pattern is a 3D function of model-space position, so it flows across facets and parts (the UV-only version was a jigsaw, evidence 00); fallbacks: UV-space mask -> plain colours -> original texture tinted by MeshPart.Color; eyes/claws/belly never painted. Species body colour now MEASURED from each texture (all 22 species recolour, the old 3-entry table is only the pre-load fallback). Bakes live in a `PatternAtlasCache` (cap 24, ref counted, LRU, keys = texture+pattern+colours+blend+tier, in-flight dedupe, chunked yields, far LOD). Fused tiers: BD-061. Verified: `tools/verify_lowpoly_patterns.py`; Studio contact sheet 4 species x 3 patterns x (base + 3 tiers) in `docs/evidence/2026-10-16-lowpoly-patterns/`. NOT verified: owner eye check, phone cost of the bakes (a few seconds per new gene set on PC), published server (EditableMesh/EditableImage need asset ownership), multiplayer, Reduce Motion. Known: 7 images per dino (3 distinct dinos textured at once, rest on the cheap tint, BD-306), `07_diamond_scales` reads as a lattice not scales (BD-307), gloss subtle in flat light (BD-308) | **Sync 2026-10-16 (PR audit):** Registry (22 species), gene patterns, Gold/Emerald/Diamond tiers, animations and atlas bake done behind Config.LowPolyDinos (PRs #142,#145,#149,#152,#165,#169). Numbers PROVISIONAL. **PROPOSAL 2026-10-17 (claude/bd245-hit-shape, for the OWNER, not a LEAD decision; offline only, Studio not used, combat OFF): the hit zone shape.** After HS1 (BD-514) the zone is a circle of `max(width, length) / 2 x growth` capped at 14: the cap bites from growth 1.94 (Ankylosaurus), 2.3 to 3.4 for most species and 3.64 for Compy, so at growth 4 and 4.8 21 of 22 species have the same 14 stud zone (a BIG 37 x 69 Ankylosaurus has a 28 x 28 zone, smaller than its body) and a long dinosaur is hit as far beside its flank as at its snout. Compared a cap that grows with the scale (huge zones, still round), `(width + length) / 4` (smaller, misses nose and tail, Compy at growth 1 changes) and a body capsule. **Recommended: the capsule**, radius `width / 2 x growth` round a spine of half-length `(length - width) / 2 x growth` (a flier, wider than long, stays a circle of half its wingspan): exactly as wide and as long as the body, strictly bigger with every growth step (each at least 15%), reaches as far lengthwise as today's uncapped rule (Compy growth 1: 3.85 both ways, sideways 1.75), no cap needed (safety limits radius 20, half-spine 24). Today's capped radius vs capsule radius+halfSpan at growth 1 / 2 / 4 / 4.8: Compy 3.8 vs 1.7+2.1 / 7.7 vs 3.5+4.2 / 14.0 vs 7.0+8.4 / 14.0 vs 8.4+10.1; Stegosaurus 5.8 vs 2.6+3.2 / 11.7 vs 5.2+6.4 / 14.0 vs 10.5+12.9 / 14.0 vs 12.6+15.5; Ankylosaurus 7.2 vs 3.9+3.3 / 14.0 vs 7.8+6.6 / 14.0 vs 15.6+13.2 / 14.0 vs 18.7+15.9; Spinosaurus 6.0 vs 2.8+3.3 / 12.1 vs 5.5+6.5 / 14.0 vs 11.1+13.1 / 14.0 vs 13.3+15.7; Mosasaurus 5.2 vs 1.7+3.5 / 10.4 vs 3.4+7.0 / 14.0 vs 6.7+14.1 / 14.0 vs 8.1+16.9; Quetzalcoatlus 4.1 vs 4.1+0 / 8.2 vs 8.2+0 / 14.0 vs 16.4+0 / 14.0 vs 19.7+0. | BD-306 | Review / test | P1 | Low-poly image budget: a dino costs 7 EditableImages (one per part), so only 3 distinct patterned dinos fit the cap of 24 and the rest show the cheap tint. Regenerate or re-bake the 22 species with ONE texture atlas per species (one image per dino, 30 on screen like BD-203), or merge parts into one MeshPart with bones. Measure on a phone (BD-168). **2026-10-16 (claude/lowpoly-atlas, flag still off, docs/105 s3):** measured in the Play client: the limit is a shared **80 MiB byte budget** for EditableImage AND EditableMesh (count irrelevant; the old "273 images" was an Edit-mode artefact; a mesh costs ~1 KiB per face, a Compy body 3 MiB). The one-atlas-per-dino option (a) was built (one 512 px image + per-species remapped EditableMesh copies via `ApplyMesh`, published ids untouched, evidence 00) and REJECTED on that measurement: it spends 4.5..9.7 MiB of kept meshes per species to save 0.75 MiB of images per dino (5 species ate 38 MiB). Chosen: sized per-part bakes, body/head 256 px, other parts 128 px = about 0.8 MiB per distinct dino, no mesh kept; caps `BigCap 48` + `SmallCap 120` = 24 distinct dinos, under half the budget with reads in flight (`tools/verify_lowpoly_budget.py`); source reads throttled to 2 at a time (12 parallel reads starved the budget in the first run), nil image fails the bake, failed reads retried after 20 s, near dinos on the tint retry every 10 s. Play client, 12 distinct species x gene sets: all 12 fully textured, 84 images, 9.75 MiB, budget 80 -> 70 MiB free, 38.5 s for the batch (evidence 06, README table). NOT verified: phone, published place (BD-309), owner eye check **Board audit 2026-10-16:** Merged: #169, #197. | BD-225, BD-203 | docs/105 s3 **Left (board hygiene 2026-10-17):** phone memory measurement (BD-309 published place, BD-085); owner eye. |

| BD-324 | Review / test | P2 | Mixed look in the playtest: world NPC predators stayed on the legacy visuals while players were low-poly. **Done 2026-10-16 (branch claude/npc-lowpoly):** with Config.LowPolyDinos on, NpcPredatorService clones the species low-poly model (LowPolyDinos.clone, same part rules), welds it with ResourceWeld and tints it dark with a thin translucent shell per textured part (no EditableImage bake, no extra image memory; pure rules in Shared/NpcLowPoly). LowPolyAnimator animates models flagged NpcLowPoly with the same LowPolyPose functions (idle/walk from velocity, new windup lean and bite lunge phases from the replicated NpcCue/BiteSeq). Legacy visual stays when the flag is off, the species is unmapped or the model did not load (checked in Studio by deleting the templates). **Studio finding:** plain MeshPart.Color is ignored on a textured MeshPart (so LowPolyDinos tintColor, the cheap fallback, does nothing visible in Studio) and SurfaceAppearance.ColorMap cannot be written by a game script. **Not done:** the egg visual; the real chase/windup/bite sequence was not played in Studio (the NPC did not aggro the test player), the windup/bite poses were driven by setting NpcCue/BiteSeq on the client and read back from the weld C1 values; no phone or published-server test. Proven by `verify_multiplayer_sim.py` (spammer at 10 Hz x 3 remotes, 12 players, also at 30% failures: 11 other settlements committed, spammer key about 90 writes vs 257 unbudgeted, peak about 100 requests/min vs 180). | BD-381 | Review / test | P2 | Smoke (113): the Sanctuary dino (HOME card and pad) renders as a blocky legacy model while the island dino is low-poly; Fusion shows an "Older dino / can't" card for the starter. Accept: one consistent look in the playtest profile. **Fix 2026-10-17 (branch claude/bd381-lowpoly-home):** ROOT CAUSE (reproduced in Studio, playtest build): the flag was fine (baked into Shared/Config, read only in `LowPoly.enabled`), but the 22 low-poly templates are loaded by the server with `InsertService:LoadAsset` one after the other (about 25 s) and replicate late. `ResourceModels.clone` returned nil while a template was missing, so every ViewportFrame card (all go through `DinosaurPreview`: HOME/Hatch card, Dino Details, Index, My dinos, Fusion list and slots, shop headers) silently fell back to the legacy model and KEPT it after the template arrived (a probe preview built with the template missing still showed the legacy Compy 3 s after it was restored). The world character and pad already self-heal (ResourceVisuals ChildAdded + BD-389 ladder; re-checked in Studio). Fix: new pure `LowPolyReady` rule + `LowPoly.pending/whenReady`; a patient (preview) clone returns nil while the low-poly model is pending, the card shows the flat picture and rebuilds the moment the template lands (retries while pending do not use the attempt budget); legacy stays only for a real failure (loader done, no template) or flag off; the loader now loads the starter compy first and the other 21 concurrently. New `tools/verify_lowpoly_views.py` (CI) fails if any view path can take a legacy model while the flag is on (static path audit plus the real `ResourceModels.clone` run with stubs). **Fusion "Older dino":** correct by design, NOT changed: the starter Compy has no saved genes/pattern (`legacy_unknown`, bucket without genes), and the server and `Sel.ineligibility` require `genes.patternId`, so it can never be fused; no profile rewrite or migration. Only the red card words changed from "No pattern: can't fuse" to "Hatch new dinos to fuse" (card line above stays "Older dino"; docs/72 kid words: what to do, no rule word). Evidence: docs/evidence/2026-10-17-bd381/. **Board audit 2026-10-16:** #210 did not change this (cause not found). | BD-324 |

| BD-397 | Review / test | P0 | **2026-10-16 Fable (branch claude/hatch-redesign): Phase 1 design [docs/118](118-hatch-redesign.md) (beat sheet 6.7 s, camera/layers, asset prompts A-E, paint-on-egg bake design, fallback chain, phone budget) and Phase 2 code behind `Config.HatchV2 = false` (3D generated egg in a ViewportFrame, two 3D buckets (PLACEHOLDER hub barrel until the bucket mesh exists), liquid arc / crown / drips as tinted fx3 sprites, two paint coats baked on the egg texture by one EditableImage: base coat floods from splat points with drips, accent coat only on the gene pattern, wet gloss dries, condition overlay, rattle, then the unchanged v1 burst and reveal; Reduce Motion, skip, v1 fallback when meshes/images are unavailable). Pure logic tested by `tools/verify_hatch_v2.py`; Studio stills in docs/evidence/2026-10-16-hatch-v2 (see its README for the honest rating and what still needs owner assets: bucket mesh, paint FX sheets, crack decal). Not verified: phone, published place, sound.** Original brief: Hatching experience redesign (owner 2026-10-16, assign to Fable; screenshots show the current look: cracked elliptical egg, flat grey bucket icon, a few dots/circles as splash that do not cover the egg).** Owner: it looks code-generated; it must be state-of-the-art Roblox quality and fit the game vision (docs/87). No elliptical/primitive shapes. Required beats: (1) the FIRST bucket appears (real designed bucket asset, not a flat icon) on the left, labelled with the base colour and its rarity (e.g. Blue - Common), (2) it tips and PAINT-SPLASHES the whole egg: real paint effect: thick liquid arcs, drops, drips running down, glossy wet look, the egg is fully coated in that colour; (3) the SECOND bucket appears on the right with the accent colour (e.g. Black - Mythic), (4) its splash uses the egg's PATTERN as the mask: the accent paint lands only on the pattern spots/stripes while the base colour stays, and the player visibly sees the new pattern being painted (paint spreads, drips, dries into the pattern), (5) the Cracked/Dirty condition cracks and overlays, then the crack and reveal of the dino. Mechanics/physics: believable paint physics (arc trajectory, splash crown, drips, settle), squash and stretch of the egg, soft camera, calm timing, Reduce Motion variant, skip option. Art rule (AGENTS.md): NO code/Python-drawn final art: bucket and egg are real meshes (Roblox generate_mesh, or Blender renders / owner ChatGPT images), splash/drip/droplet FX are flipbook textures with alpha (owner-made in ChatGPT, processed to white-on-alpha, tinted at runtime) plus ParticleEmitters, and the paint-on-egg result is a baked texture mask (reuse the LowPolyTexture/PatternAtlas bake approach with the egg's pattern and two colours, cached, under the EditableImage budget docs/105). Deliverables: design doc with beat sheet and timings, asset list with ready prompts (bucket mesh prompt, splash/drip flipbook ChatGPT prompts per docs/109), the implementation plan in small tasks, then the implementation behind a flag with fallback to the current sequence, tests and Studio before/after evidence. | BD-590 | Review / test | P3 | **Admin Take back accepts any gift id.** In Studio a Take back of an id that was never sent ("nope") answered "revoked" and wrote a tombstone (`claimedGifts[id] = "revoked"`, audit line) that also counts toward the claimedGifts cap, so an admin typo looks like success. Server + page copy + verify_gifts. **Done 2026-10-17 (offline, no Studio; flags stay off):** `GiftService.revoke` now confirms the id first: pending in the recipient's inbox, or in their audit trail as sent (`admin`, `queued`, `bought`) or opened (`claimed`). Otherwise it answers `unknown_gift` and writes nothing (no tombstone, no `claimedGifts` slot, no audit line; one `[BD-GIFT]` log). An id that does not start with `A` or `R` (or has odd characters) is `bad_request` before any store read. A repeat of a real take back is `already_revoked`, also after the recipient's claim moved the tombstone to the profile (the trail remembers it). An unreadable trail answers `try_again`, never `unknown_gift`. Admin page copy: "No gift with that number." Limit: the trail keeps the last 50 events per player, so an opened gift older than that cannot be found. `verify_gifts` covers all of it. Studio recheck of the typo case rides on BD-589/BD-591. | BD-579, BD-452 |

| BD-595 | Done | P2 | **Fly hover cap: apply it on the client, measured at the feet (LEAD decision FH1, [115](115-decisions-log.md); brief [101](101-hp-combat-classes-animations.md) s20).** The server hover loop in `ClassMovementService` never acted (the character is owned by its client). Do: a pure `ClassMovement.hoverStep` (feet height = root height above ground minus `HipHeight + root.Size.Y / 2`, cap 6.5 stays, not applied while `LeapUntil` is running), called from `ClassGlide.client.luau` next to the glide; delete the dead server hover loop; verifier cases in `tools/verify_class_movement.py` (a Fly jump of 6.05 studs is NOT cut, a leap of 10.4 is NOT cut, a boosted jump power IS held at 6.5, mutants: root-centre measure, leap not exempt, cap off). No number changes; Fly must still rise above Ground (6.05 vs 5.0). Studio re-measure: peak feet height 6.05, leap unchanged. Options B (cap about 10 on the root) and C (no cap) were rejected in s20. **Built 2026-10-17 (option A, offline; Studio numbers pending):** pure `ClassMovement.feetHeight` / `hoverStep` (cap = the class table's `heightCap` 6.5, no new number; it can only lower an upward speed, and holds a rise so the feet end at the cap), applied in `ClassGlide.client.luau` in the same single velocity write as the glide (ground by one short ray, only while rising in the air; skipped while the local `LeapPredictAt` stamp or the server's `LeapUntil` says a leap runs); the dead server hover/glide loop in `ClassMovementService` is deleted (the `ClassGlide` remote stays, it only records the flag). `tools/verify_class_movement.py` simulates the physics step plus Heartbeat: plain Fly jump apex unchanged (5.60 in the 60 Hz simulation, 6.05 continuous), boosted jump (jump power 165) held at 6.43 (uncapped 53), leap unchanged (9.78; 6.43 without the exemption), glide still 12 studs/s after a capped jump, mutants (root-centre measure, no leap exemption, adds upward velocity, pushes up at the cap, cap off) all caught. **Studio measured 2026-10-17 ([evidence](evidence/2026-10-17-hover/README.md), Heartbeat samples, real equip path, island run):** boosted jump (jump power 99) held at **6.45 to 6.48** (22.24 with the cap off), leap apex **10.21** (exempt), glide plateau **-16.2** studs/s (12 + one gravity step, as in BD-593), picture 01. **Not as expected: the plain Fly jump rises 6.92 uncapped (not 6.05; the offline simulation under-predicts by about 1.3), so the cap trims it to 6.45, about 0.47 stud.** Status stays Review / test, not Done: acceptance item 'a plain Fly jump is untouched' does not hold literally. **Left (LEAD decision, no code changed):** (a) keep 6.5 and reword FH1 / s12 to 'about 6.5' (recommended: not felt, no new number, keeps the 7-stud bite margin), or (b) raise `heightCap` to about 7.0 and fix the simulation in `tools/verify_class_movement.py` to the measured 6.92. | BD-593 **Lead 2026-10-17 (FH2):** Done. Option (a) taken on the Studio numbers: cap stays 6.5, plain jump 6.46 (trim 0.47, unfelt), boosted capped, leap exempt, glide 12/s; docs/101 s20.6. |

| BD-479 | Review / test | P2 | Nest weather = weather when the nest was FOUND (owner BD-235 2026-10-16; docs/99 G4). Today `Bootstrap.server.luau` passes `workspace:GetAttribute("WeatherId")` at settlement to `ProfileRepository.commitRunSettlement`, which stores that one id on every nest earned that run (`nestWeatherId`, BD-156), so a run in clear weather settled during a storm gives mutation chances and the reverse loses them. Change: record the weather active when each nest is earned (the catch or time that qualifies it), carry it in the settlement snapshot (like `RunEggTags` does for eggs: a pure module with a test in `tools/verify_mutations.py` / `verify_nests.py`), store it per nest, keep the same chance as run eggs; the duplicate-settlement probe call in `Bootstrap.server.luau` (no weather argument) and `pendingSettlements` retries must carry it too. Additive nest field, no schema bump, old nests keep what they have. Do not change odds. Needs a Studio check with the admin panel (docs/79) **Built 2026-10-16 (claude/bd-479-nest-weather, offline):** `RewardMath.recordNestWeather` / `nestWeatherId` (pure); `RunLifecycle` keeps a server-only per-run ledger (one weather id per nest ordinal, "clear" = none), filled by `RunLifecycle.noteNestWeather` right after every `CatchScore` change in `FoodService` (clear weather too) and `PredationService` (kill eggs) and by a `task.delay` at `Config.NestMinRunSeconds`, then topped up in `settlementSnapshot` with the weather at that moment for any nest not noted (the old behaviour as a fallback); the snapshot carries `nestWeather`, `Bootstrap.server.luau` passes it (no settlement-time `WeatherId` read) to `commitRunSettlement` and to the duplicate probe with the same arguments, `pendingSettlements` stores and replays it, and nest N is stamped with ledger entry N (a legacy single weather id still applies to every nest; forged, `clear`, missing or longer entries store nothing; capacity-blocked nests lose their entry). Same mutation chance, no schema bump, additive `weatherId` field only, existing saves untouched. Tests: `tools/verify_gameplay.py` (rain/clear nests settled in blizzard, fallback, short run, disconnect), `tools/verify_nests.py` (per-nest tags, forged/short/legacy, duplicate and probe never re-stamp, ack-lost, transient and parked-then-renewed retries exactly once, capacity), `tools/verify_mutations.py` source scans. **Left:** lead Studio check, [141](141-board-hygiene-stuck-rows.md) script B (mutations): admin weather rain, collect eggs until the first nest qualifies (about 2 min and one catch), switch weather, claim the nest and confirm its eggs mutate only for the weather it was found in. | BD-235, BD-156 | docs/99 G4 |

Result: every species at scale 1, 2 and 4 now fills 26% to 56% of the screen height, in frame, top margin 165 px or more, bottom margin 71 px or more, ground ahead visible, no HUD box over the dino (66 shots; `tools/verify_camera_framing.py` in CI keeps module and sweep in step). Options: keep (flat, simple, no species advantage) or reach = base + 0.4 x half the body length (a small bonus for long bodies; growth speed impact needs the food-per-minute measurement of BD-471). Built.** `FoodService` measures the pickup from the head point (root + flat forward x headOffset, the centre of the head, from the Studio-measured `snout` and `headLength` of each species in `ScaleLadder`) with a radius that follows the HEAD size plus the old growth bonus: a Compy keeps 2.25 at scale 1 and stays within 5% at scales 1, 2, 4; every species reaches about 1.4 studs past its nose; a berry 1 stud behind the tail or under the chest is not eaten. One pure function, `src/shared/MouthReach.luau`, for the server and any client preview; the pickup grid (BD-361), rate limits, vertical tolerance and respawn are unchanged; only with `LowPolyDinos` on (otherwise the Build 020 root rule). Predation gets the same head point behind `Config.PredationAtMouth` = false (instant predation is live and a bite feel needs two players; offline verified only). Verified: `tools/verify_mouth_reach.py` (22 species x scale 1, 2, 4, 4.8 x 4 directions; Compy within 10%; one shared function; flags), `verify_gameplay`, `verify_scale_ladder`, `verify_sources`. Studio (own file `dtmp/mouth.rbxlx`, own window): Compy, Ankylosaurus (scale 1 and 2) and Spinosaurus eating a berry at the mouth with real server pickups, a real Humanoid walk, and the chest control, [docs/evidence/2026-10-17-mouth-pickup/](evidence/2026-10-17-mouth-pickup/README.md). Not verified: phone, published server, two players, fliers and swimmers in a real run. **Update (BD-522, MR5): the Compy "keeps 2.25 / within 5%" statement above is superseded: slack 0.25 and radius pad 1.0 (Compy radius 1.83 at scale 1) so the mouth rule does not speed up food.** Follow-ups BD-520, BD-521, BD-522 | BD-500, BD-471 |

| BD-618 | Review / test | P3 | **Remote hygiene bundle (docs/151 F8).** (a) `ClassGlide` stores a flag nobody reads since BD-593 (the client still sends it): drop the store or the remote. (b) `ClientWorldReady` is believed: a client skips the 20 s terrain wait (self only). (c) `PrivateState` hello sends a full snapshot (about 10 KB after long play) every 0.5 s: raise the floor to 2 s. (d) `EquipSpecies` answering `already_equipped` still calls `refreshForPlayer`: skip it. (e) Public attributes say more than the UI needs: `IsAdmin` (who the admin is), `LastPredatorUserId` (who ate you), `LastNestClaimAt` (the server's `os.clock()`): move them to `PrivateState` or drop them. (f) Limiters keyed by Player object reset on a rejoin (`FeedbackService`, `FusionService`, `ObjectiveService`, `ProgressionService`, the Home cooldown): use `CooldownMemory` where it matters (log volume and cheap requests only today). (g) `DemoTriggerService.placeOnGround` does not check x, z, fx, fz for NaN (Studio only). (h) The operation ring keeps 50 entries, so a purchase token replayed after 50 newer operations runs again (it costs again; no gain). (i) `SkinService` (BD-456, flag off) writes through `applySkin` without `admit`: count it in the server-wide share. One verifier line per item as it is fixed. **2026-10-18 (offline evidence only, `hygiene-618` scenario in `tools/verify_remote_fuzz.py`, 23 checks; the checks for (a) to (d) were confirmed to FAIL on the old code).** (a) DONE: no `OnServerEvent` handler and no store for `ClassGlide` any more; the RemoteEvent stays only as the "class rules are on" marker the client waits for, and `ClassGlide.client.luau` no longer fires it (the Studio-only `DemoDirector` glide trigger still fires it harmlessly). (b) DONE as far as the server can: `ClientWorldReady` is believed once, and only after `IslandTerrainReady` on the server; a client that lies after that still only skips its own terrain wait (the server cannot see client terrain). (c) DONE: hello floor raised from 0.5 s to 2 s (`PrivateState.HelloGapSeconds`); a request inside the floor is answered by ONE trailing snapshot, so a resync after a missed delta is never lost. (d) DONE: `already_equipped` does not call `refreshForPlayer`. (e) PARTLY: `LastPredatorUserId` is no longer a Player attribute (server memory, `RunLifecycle.lastPredatorUserId`); `LastNestClaimAt` is a per-player counter instead of `os.clock()`; **`IsAdmin` stays public on purpose**: the admin panel and the HUD debug line read it as an attribute, moving it needs client changes that cannot be checked without Studio (follow-up if wanted). (f) DONE for the five named: Home cooldown (`CooldownMemory`), `FusionService` and `ObjectiveService` windows (keyed by UserId, kept after leaving, pruned), `ProgressionService` 0.4 s gap (UserId), `FeedbackService` session limits (UserId); a rejoin no longer resets them. (g) DONE: `placeOnGround` refuses NaN, infinite and absurd x, z, fx, fz. (h) DONE without a schema change: purchase tokens committed this server are also kept in memory (400 per user), so a replay after the 50-entry ring dropped the token answers `duplicate` (or `token_conflict` for another item) and charges nothing; the ring itself is unchanged. (i) DONE: `ProfileRepository.applySkin` calls `admit` (moved to the budgeted set in the verifier). No profile field added or changed, no flag changed. **Not verified:** Studio, a second client, the admin panel, a published server. | BD-608, BD-363 |
